```markdown
# STUDYFORGE — COMPLETE SINGLE HTML FILE IMPLEMENTATION

## YOUR ROLE

You are an autonomous AI agent. You will plan, design, and build
the complete StudyForge web application as a single HTML file.

You will read all attached documents carefully before planning.
You will plan every stage with clear inputs and outputs.
Then you will build the complete implementation in one file.

You work independently. No mid-build guidance will be given.
Plan everything first. Then build everything. Deliver one file.

---

## CRITICAL — AI INTEGRATION FUNCTIONS

You will NOT write your own API calls.
You will NOT ask for API keys.
You will NOT use fetch directly for AI features.

The following three functions are pre-built and ready to use.
Copy them exactly into your code and call them wherever AI is needed.

```javascript
// ─── FUNCTION 1: Text Generation & Analysis ───────────────────
async function geminiGenerateText(promptText) {
    const apiKey = "";
    const apiUrl = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-3-flash-preview:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`;
    const response = await fetch(apiUrl, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            contents: [{ parts: [{ text: promptText }] }]
        })
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`API Error: ${response.status}`);
    const result = await response.json();
    return result?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || "No text generated.";
}

// ─── FUNCTION 2: Live Web Search (Grounding) ──────────────────
async function geminiSearchText(searchQuery) {
    const apiKey = "";
    const apiUrl = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-3-flash-preview:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`;
    const response = await fetch(apiUrl, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            contents: [{ parts: [{ text: `Search the web for: "${searchQuery}". Provide a concise summary.` }] }],
            tools: [{ googleSearch: {} }]
        })
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`API Error: ${response.status}`);
    const result = await response.json();
    return result?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || "No search results.";
}

// ─── FUNCTION 3: Image Generation ─────────────────────────────
async function geminiImageCreation(imagePrompt) {
    const apiKey = "";
    const apiUrl = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/imagen-4.0-generate-001:predict?key=${apiKey}`;
    const response = await fetch(apiUrl, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            instances: [{ prompt: imagePrompt }],
            parameters: { sampleCount: 1, aspectRatio: "16:9" }
        })
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`API Error: ${response.status}`);
    const result = await response.json();
    const base64Encoded = result?.predictions?.[0]?.bytesBase64Encoded;
    if (!base64Encoded) throw new Error("No image data returned.");
    return `data:image/png;base64,${base64Encoded}`;
}
```

### How to use these functions

```
geminiGenerateText(prompt)
  → Use for: material analysis, blueprint generation,
             note writing, validation, AI chat responses,
             auto-drafting text, prompt regeneration
  → Always await and wrap in try/catch
  → Output is a markdown string — render with your parser

geminiSearchText(query)
  → Use for: current affairs search (national/state/international),
             personality news lookups, location news,
             any real-time information needed
  → Always await and wrap in try/catch
  → Output is a markdown string

geminiImageCreation(prompt)
  → Use for: generating all study images, infographics,
             diagrams, visualize-this feature
  → Always await and wrap in try/catch
  → Output is a base64 data URL — use directly in <img src="">
```

## WHAT YOU ARE BUILDING

StudyForge transforms study material into structured notes using AI.

A user uploads a file, types a topic, or pastes text.
The app then:

- Reads and understands the material
- Detects which content features apply
- Splits content into logical parts
- Creates a teaching blueprint per part
- Generates formatted study notes with visuals
- Searches and integrates current affairs
- Validates the generated content
- Exports the final document

This is a real working application. All AI features use the
three functions provided above. No mocks. No dummy data.
Everything must work when opened in Gemini Canvas.

       SECTION 5: SHARED UI COMPONENTS
       ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */
    // Button, Modal, Toggle, Card, Badge,
    // ProgressBar, MarkdownContent, SourceTag,
    // ContainerRenderer, LoadingSpinner

    // EditPartModal, AIChat

    const App = () => { ... };

    /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════
       SECTION 10: RENDER
       ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */
    const root = ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root'));
    root.render(<App />);
  </script>
</body>
</html>

## GLOBAL NAVIGATION SYSTEM

```javascript
// Navigation state
const [currentScreen, setCurrentScreen] = useState(1);
const [maxReachedScreen, setMaxReachedScreen] = useState(1);

// Forward navigation — only allowed to next unlocked screen
const navigateForward = (targetScreen) => {
  if (targetScreen <= maxReachedScreen + 1) {
    setCurrentScreen(targetScreen);
    if (targetScreen > maxReachedScreen) {
      setMaxReachedScreen(targetScreen);
    }
    saveState();
  }
};

// Backward navigation — always allowed to any completed screen
const navigateBack = (targetScreen) => {
  if (targetScreen <= maxReachedScreen) {
    setCurrentScreen(targetScreen);
  }
};
```

### Top Navigation Bar (visible on every screen)

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 📚 StudyForge  │  [Screen Name]  │  Step ①②③④⑤⑥⑦⑧  │  Controls │
│                │                 │                   │           │
│ Logo + Name    │ Current stage   │ Clickable steps   │ [Prompts] │
│                │                 │ ✓ = done          │ [Help]    │
│                │                 │ ● = current       │ [Reset]   │
│                │                 │ ○ = locked        │ [Saved ✓] │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

```jsx
const TopBar = ({ currentScreen, maxReachedScreen, navigateBack,
                  onPromptCenter, onHelp, onReset, saveStatus }) => (
  <div className="sticky top-0 z-50 bg-white border-b border-gray-200
                  px-6 py-3 flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm">

    {/* Left: Logo */}
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
      <span className="text-xl font-bold text-blue-600">📚 StudyForge</span>
      <span className="text-gray-300">|</span>
      <span className="text-sm text-gray-600 font-medium">
        {SCREEN_NAMES[currentScreen]}
      </span>
    </div>

    {/* Center: Step indicators */}
    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
      {[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8].map(n => (
        <button
          key={n}
          onClick={() => n <= maxReachedScreen && navigateBack(n)}
          title={SCREEN_NAMES[n]}
          className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-all
            ${currentScreen === n
              ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white ring-2 ring-blue-300'
              : n < maxReachedScreen
              ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700 hover:bg-green-200 cursor-pointer'
              : n === maxReachedScreen
              ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 cursor-pointer'
              : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed'}`}
        >
          {n < maxReachedScreen ? '✓' : n}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>

    {/* Right: Controls */}
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
      <AutoSaveIndicator status={saveStatus} />
      <button onClick={onPromptCenter}
        className="text-sm text-gray-600 hover:text-blue-600 px-2 py-1">
        🔧 Prompts
      </button>
      <button onClick={onHelp}
        className="text-sm text-gray-600 hover:text-blue-600 px-2 py-1">
        ❓ Help
      </button>
      <button onClick={onReset}
        className="text-sm text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 px-2 py-1">
        🔄 Reset
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
);
```

---

## AUTO-SAVE SYSTEM

```javascript
// Debounced auto-save — triggers 1 second after any state change
const saveTimerRef = useRef(null);
const [saveStatus, setSaveStatus] = useState('saved');

const triggerAutoSave = (stateSnapshot) => {
  setSaveStatus('saving');
  if (saveTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(saveTimerRef.current);
  saveTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
    try {
      // Strip large base64 image data before saving to avoid quota
      const saveable = {
        ...stateSnapshot,
        generated: Object.fromEntries(
          Object.entries(stateSnapshot.generated || {}).map(([k, v]) => [
            k, { ...v, images: (v.images || []).map(img => ({
              ...img, url: null  // Don't save base64 to localStorage
            }))}
          ])
        )
      };
      localStorage.setItem('studyforge_session', JSON.stringify(saveable));
      setSaveStatus('saved');
    } catch (e) {
      setSaveStatus('error');
      console.warn('Save failed:', e.message);
    }
  }, 1000);
};

// Auto-Save Indicator Component
const AutoSaveIndicator = ({ status }) => (
  <span className={`text-xs font-medium px-2 py-1 rounded-full
    ${status === 'saving' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700' :
      status === 'saved'  ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
                            'bg-red-100 text-red-600'}`}>
    {status === 'saving' ? '💾 Saving...' :
     status === 'saved'  ? '✅ Saved' : '⚠️ Save failed'}
  </span>
);
```

## SESSION RESTORE (Gap #3 Fix)

```javascript
// On app mount — check for saved session
useEffect(() => {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('studyforge_session');
  if (saved) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
      // Set all state from saved session
      setAppState(parsed);
      setCurrentScreen(parsed.currentScreen || 1);
      setMaxReachedScreen(parsed.maxReachedScreen || 1);
      setShowResumePrompt(true); // Show resume banner
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Could not restore session');
    }
  }
}, []);

// Resume Banner — shown at top of Screen 1 if session found
const ResumeBanner = ({ savedState, onResume, onFresh }) => (
  <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6
                  flex items-center justify-between">
    <div>
      <p className="font-semibold text-blue-800">
        📂 Saved session found: "{savedState.input?.topic || 'Previous session'}"
      </p>
      <p className="text-sm text-blue-600">
        Last saved: {new Date(savedState.lastSaved).toLocaleString()}
      </p>
    </div>
    <div className="flex gap-3">
      <button onClick={onResume}
        className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-medium
                   hover:bg-blue-700">
        Resume Session
      </button>
      <button onClick={onFresh}
        className="bg-white border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-4 py-2
                   rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-gray-50">
        Start Fresh
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
);

// When Resume is clicked:
// Repopulate Screen 1 inputs, topic field, sub-topics array,
// paste textarea, all optional settings, file name display
// Navigate directly to the screen user was on
```

### Section A — File Upload

```jsx
const FileUploadSection = ({ onFileLoad }) => {
  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
  const [fileInfo, setFileInfo] = useState(null);

  const handleFile = (file) => {
    const allowed = [
      'application/pdf', 'text/plain', 'text/markdown',
      'text/html', 'application/msword',
      'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document',
      'image/jpeg', 'image/png'
    ];
    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
    const allowedExt = ['pdf','txt','doc','docx','md','html','jpg','jpeg','png'];

    if (!allowedExt.includes(ext)) {
      alert('File type not supported. Use PDF, TXT, DOC, DOCX, MD, HTML, JPG, PNG');
      return;
    }

    setFileInfo({
      name: file.name,
      size: (file.size / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB',
      type: ext.toUpperCase(),
      rawFile: file
    });

    // Read file content
    const reader = new FileReader();
    if (['jpg','jpeg','png'].includes(ext)) {
      reader.readAsDataURL(file); // base64 for images
    } else {
      reader.readAsText(file);
    }
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      onFileLoad({
        name: file.name,
        size: file.size,
        type: ext,
        content: e.target.result
      });
    };
  };

  return (
    <div
      onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); }}
      onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
      onDrop={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(false);
                       handleFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]); }}
      className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg p-8 text-center
                  transition-colors cursor-pointer
                  ${dragOver ? 'border-blue-400 bg-blue-50'
                             : 'border-gray-300 hover:border-gray-400'}`}
      onClick={() => document.getElementById('file-input').click()}
    >
      <input id="file-input" type="file" className="hidden"
        accept=".pdf,.txt,.doc,.docx,.md,.html,.jpg,.jpeg,.png"
        onChange={(e) => handleFile(e.target.files[0])} />

      {fileInfo ? (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4">
          <span className="text-3xl">📄</span>
          <div className="text-left">
            <p className="font-semibold text-gray-800">{fileInfo.name}</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              {fileInfo.size} · {fileInfo.type}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setFileInfo(null); }}
            className="ml-4 text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 text-sm font-medium">
            Remove
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <span className="text-4xl mb-3 block">📁</span>
          <p className="text-gray-700 font-medium">Drop file here or click to browse</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">
            PDF · TXT · DOC · DOCX · MD · HTML · JPG · PNG
          </p>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### Section B — Topic Input (Gap #2 Fix — Dynamic Sub-topics)

```jsx
const TopicInputSection = ({ topicData, onChange, onAIDraft }) => {
  const [subTopics, setSubTopics] = useState(topicData.subTopics || ['', '', '']);
  const [isDrafting, setIsDrafting] = useState(false);

  const addSubTopic = () => {
    if (subTopics.length < 10) {
      const updated = [...subTopics, ''];
      setSubTopics(updated);
      onChange({ subTopics: updated });
    }
  };

  const removeSubTopic = (index) => {
    const updated = subTopics.filter((_, i) => i !== index);
    setSubTopics(updated);
    onChange({ subTopics: updated });
  };

  const updateSubTopic = (index, value) => {
    const updated = [...subTopics];
    updated[index] = value;
    setSubTopics(updated);
    onChange({ subTopics: updated });
  };

  // AI Draft Text (Gap #4 Fix)
  const handleAIDraft = async () => {
    if (!topicData.topic.trim()) return;
    setIsDrafting(true);
    try {
      const prompt = `Write comprehensive study material about: ${topicData.topic}.
        Sub-topics to cover: ${subTopics.filter(Boolean).join(', ')}.
        ${topicData.focus ? `Focus: ${topicData.focus}` : ''}
        Write in clear, educational style. Include definitions, examples, and key facts.
        Length: 400-600 words.`;

      const drafted = await geminiGenerateText(prompt);
      onAIDraft(drafted); // Injects into Section C textarea
    } catch (e) {
      alert('Could not draft text. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setIsDrafting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Main Topic */}
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
          Main Topic <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
        </label>
        <input type="text" value={topicData.topic}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ topic: e.target.value })}
          placeholder="e.g., National Income Accounting"
          className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                     focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none" />
      </div>

      {/* Dynamic Sub-topics */}
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
          Sub-topics <span className="text-gray-400">(optional, up to 10)</span>
        </label>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {subTopics.map((st, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex gap-2">
              <input type="text" value={st}
                onChange={(e) => updateSubTopic(i, e.target.value)}
                placeholder={`Sub-topic ${i + 1}`}
                className="flex-1 border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                           focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none text-sm" />
              <button onClick={() => removeSubTopic(i)}
                className="text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 px-2 font-medium">
                ×
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        {subTopics.length < 10 && (
          <button onClick={addSubTopic}
            className="mt-2 text-sm text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium">
            + Add Sub-topic
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Specific Focus */}
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
          Specific Focus <span className="text-gray-400">(optional)</span>
        </label>
        <textarea rows={2} value={topicData.focus}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ focus: e.target.value })}
          placeholder="e.g., Focus on MP state data and PYQs for MPPSC 2025"
          className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                     focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none text-sm resize-none" />
      </div>

      {/* AI Draft Button — appears when topic is filled */}
      {topicData.topic.trim() && (
        <button onClick={handleAIDraft} disabled={isDrafting}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200
                     text-purple-700 hover:bg-purple-100 px-4 py-2 rounded-md font-medium
                     disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
          {isDrafting ? '⏳ Drafting...' : '✨ AI Draft Text'}
          <span className="text-xs text-purple-500">
            (auto-fills paste section below)
          </span>
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### Section C — Paste Text

```jsx
const PasteTextSection = ({ value, onChange }) => {
  const MAX_CHARS = 50000;
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
        Paste your material
      </label>
      <textarea rows={8} value={value}
        onChange={(e) => {
          if (e.target.value.length <= MAX_CHARS) onChange(e.target.value);
        }}
        placeholder="Paste notes, textbook content, articles, or any text here..."
        className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                   focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none resize-y font-mono text-sm" />
      <div className="flex justify-between mt-1">
        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">
          Any language accepted
        </span>
        <span className={`text-xs font-medium
          ${value.length > MAX_CHARS * 0.9 ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>
          {value.length.toLocaleString()} / {MAX_CHARS.toLocaleString()}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

### Continue Button + Validation

```jsx
// Validation before continuing
const handleContinue = () => {
  const hasFile = !!inputState.fileContent;
  const hasTopic = inputState.topic.trim().length > 0;
  const hasPaste = inputState.pastedText.trim().length > 0;

  if (!hasFile && !hasTopic && !hasPaste) {
    setValidationError(
      'Please upload a file, type a topic, or paste text to continue.'
    );
    return;
  }
  setValidationError('');
  navigateForward(2);
};
```

## SCREEN 2 — READING

### Purpose
Run real Gemini analysis. Show animated progress. Move to Screen 3.

### AI Call — Material Analysis

```javascript
// Build the analysis prompt
const buildAnalysisPrompt = (inputData, settings) => {
  const content = inputData.fileContent || inputData.pastedText || '';
  const topic = inputData.topic || '';
  const subTopics = (inputData.subTopics || []).filter(Boolean);

  return `
You are an expert educational content analyzer.
Analyze the following study material and return a detailed analysis.

${topic ? `TOPIC: ${topic}` : ''}
${subTopics.length ? `SUB-TOPICS: ${subTopics.join(', ')}` : ''}
${inputData.focus ? `FOCUS: ${inputData.focus}` : ''}
${settings.exam ? `EXAM TYPE: ${settings.exam}` : ''}
${settings.state ? `STATE FOCUS: ${settings.state}` : ''}

MATERIAL:
${content.substring(0, 8000)}

Analyze and return JSON with:
{
  "subject": "main subject name",
  "subDiscipline": "specific sub-field",
  "contentType": "psc_upsc|academic|programming|geography|history|science|economics|general",
  "language": "detected language",
  "complexity": "low|medium|high|very_high",
  "topics": ["topic1", "topic2", ...],
  "definitions": [{"term": "...", "definition": "..."}],
  "formulas": [{"formula": "...", "meaning": "...", "facts": ["..."]}],
  "codeBlocks": ["..."],
  "pyqs": ["question1", ...],
  "geographyMentions": ["place1", ...],
  "personalitiesMentioned": ["name1", ...],
  "institutionsMentioned": ["institution1", ...],
  "counts": {
    "topics": 0, "definitions": 0, "formulas": 0,
    "pyqs": 0, "diagrams": 0, "tables": 0,
    "examples": 0, "timelines": 0, "codeBlocks": 0
  },
  "detectedFeatures": [
    {
      "featureId": "formula_breakdown",
      "featureName": "Formula Breakdown",
      "category": "FORMULAS_MATH",
      "detected": true,
      "reason": "12 formulas found in material",
      "priority": 1
    }
  ],
  "suggestedParts": 6,
  "splitRationale": "reason for this number of parts"
}

Return ONLY valid JSON. No markdown wrapping.
  `.trim();
};
```

```javascript
// Execute analysis on Screen 2
const runAnalysis = async () => {
  const steps = [
    'Detecting input type and language...',
    'Extracting readable text content...',
    'Identifying subject and context...',
    'Analyzing structure and features...',
    'Counting elements and topics...',
    'Preparing your understanding report...'
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < steps.length; i++) {
    setCurrentStep(i);
    setProgress(Math.round((i / steps.length) * 85));
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 600));
  }

  try {
    const prompt = buildAnalysisPrompt(inputState, settingsState);
    const rawResponse = await geminiGenerateText(prompt);

    // Parse JSON — handle markdown code blocks if present
    const jsonStr = rawResponse
      .replace(/```json\n?/g, '')
      .replace(/```\n?/g, '')
      .trim();
    const analysisResult = JSON.parse(jsonStr);

    setProgress(100);
    setCurrentStep(steps.length);
    setAnalysisData(analysisResult);

    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500));
    navigateForward(3);

  } catch (e) {
    setAnalysisError('Analysis failed: ' + e.message);
  }
};

useEffect(() => { runAnalysis(); }, []); // Runs when Screen 2 mounts
```

### UI Display

```jsx
const Screen2Reading = () => {
  const steps = [
    'Detecting input type and language...',
    'Extracting readable text content...',
    'Identifying subject and context...',
    'Analyzing structure and features...',
    'Counting elements and topics...',
    'Preparing your understanding report...'
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
      <div className="max-w-lg w-full text-center space-y-8">
        <div className="text-6xl animate-pulse">📖</div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          Reading your material...
        </h1>

        {/* Step List */}
        <div className="text-left space-y-3">
          {steps.map((step, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <span className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center
                                justify-center text-sm flex-shrink-0
                ${i < currentStep ? 'bg-green-500 text-white' :
                  i === currentStep ? 'bg-blue-500 text-white' :
                  'bg-gray-200 text-gray-400'}`}>
                {i < currentStep ? '✓' :
                 i === currentStep ? '⟳' : '○'}
              </span>
              <span className={`text-sm
                ${i < currentStep ? 'text-green-700 font-medium' :
                  i === currentStep ? 'text-blue-700 font-medium' :
                  'text-gray-400'}`}>
                {step}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Progress Bar */}
        <div>
          <div className="h-3 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div className="h-full bg-blue-600 rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
              style={{ width: `${progress}%` }} />
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">{progress}% complete</p>
        </div>

        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
          This may take 30–60 seconds for large files
        </p>

        {analysisError && (
          <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
            <p className="text-red-700 text-sm">{analysisError}</p>
            <button onClick={runAnalysis}
              className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600 underline">
              Try Again
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

```javascript
const FEATURE_CATEGORIES = {
  CONTENT_CORE: {
    label: '📝 Core Content',
    alwaysShow: true,

      detailed_notes:        'Detailed Explanatory Notes',
      quick_revision:        'Quick Revision Boxes',

    }
  },
  FORMULAS_MATH: {

    features: {
      formula_breakdown:   'Formula Breakdown & Explanation',

// Determine which categories to show based on analysis
const getVisibleCategories = (analysisData, settings) => {
  return Object.entries(FEATURE_CATEGORIES).filter(([_, cat]) => {
    if (cat.alwaysShow) return true;
    if (cat.showWhen) return cat.showWhen(analysisData, settings);
    return false;
  });
};

### Layout

```jsx
const Screen3Understanding = ({ analysisData, settings, features,
                                 onToggleFeature, onContinue }) => {

                    {count || 0}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">{label}</p>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* See Full Details */}
            <button onClick={() => setShowDetails(!showDetails)}
              className="mt-4 text-sm text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium">
              {showDetails ? '▲ Hide details' : '👁 See full details'}
            </button>

            {showDetails && (
              <div className="mt-4 space-y-4 border-t pt-4">
                {/* Definitions Table */}
                {analysisData.definitions?.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                      Definitions
                    </h4>
                    <table className="w-full text-xs border-collapse">
                      <thead>
                        <tr className="bg-gray-50">
                          <th className="border border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 text-left">Term</th>
                          <th className="border border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 text-left">Definition</th>
                        </tr>
                      </thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {analysisData.definitions.slice(0,8).map((d, i) => (
                          <tr key={i} className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                            <td className="border border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 font-medium">
                              {d.term}
                            </td>
                            <td className="border border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 text-gray-600">
                              {d.definition}
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Formulas Table */}
                {analysisData.formulas?.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                      Formulas
                    </h4>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      {analysisData.formulas.slice(0,5).map((f, i) => (
                        <div key={i}
                          className="bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200
                                     rounded p-2">
                          <p className="font-mono text-sm font-bold text-indigo-800">
                            {f.formula}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-indigo-600 mt-1">
                            {f.meaning}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Prompt Visibility (Gap #7) */}
                <details className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                  <summary className="cursor-pointer hover:text-gray-600">
                    🔍 View analysis prompt used
                  </summary>
                  <div className="mt-2 bg-gray-50 border rounded p-3 font-mono
                                  text-xs whitespace-pre-wrap max-h-40 overflow-y-auto">
                    {buildAnalysisPrompt(inputState, settings)}
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() =>
                    navigator.clipboard.writeText(
                      buildAnalysisPrompt(inputState, settings)
                    )}
                    className="mt-1 text-blue-500 hover:text-blue-700">
                    📋 Copy prompt
                  </button>
                </details>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* RIGHT — 2/5 width */}
        <div className="col-span-2">
          <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-5
                          shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-4">
              Features Detected
            </h3>

            <div className="space-y-5">
              {visibleCategories.map(([catId, cat]) => (
                <div key={catId}>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase
                                tracking-wide mb-2">
                    {cat.label}
                  </p>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {Object.entries(cat.features).map(([featId, featName]) => {
                      const detected = analysisData.detectedFeatures
                        ?.find(f => f.featureId === featId);
                      const isOn = features[featId] ?? detected?.detected ?? false;

                      return (
                        <div key={featId}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                          <div className="flex-1">
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-700">{featName}</p>
                            {detected?.reason && (
                              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                                {detected.reason}
                              </p>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <ToggleSwitch value={isOn}
                            onChange={(v) => onToggleFeature(featId, v)} />
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}

              {/* Show more categories */}
              {hiddenCategories.length > 0 && (
                <>
                  <button onClick={() => setShowMoreCategories(!showMoreCategories)}
                    className="text-sm text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium">
                    {showMoreCategories
                      ? '▲ Show fewer categories'
                      : `+ Show ${hiddenCategories.length} more categories`}
                  </button>

                  {showMoreCategories && hiddenCategories.map(([catId, cat]) => (
                    <div key={catId}>
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase
                                    tracking-wide mb-2">
                        {cat.label}
                      </p>
                      <div className="space-y-2">
                        {Object.entries(cat.features).map(([featId, featName]) => (
                          <div key={featId}
                            className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">{featName}</p>
                            <ToggleSwitch
                              value={features[featId] ?? false}
                              onChange={(v) => onToggleFeature(featId, v)} />
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Actions */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mt-8">
        <button onClick={() => navigateBack(1)}
          className="text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 font-medium">
          ← Re-analyze
        </button>
        <button onClick={onContinue}
          className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-6 py-3
                     rounded-md font-semibold ml-auto">
          Looks Good → Continue
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

### Edit Part Modal (Gap #6 Fix — Real Modal)

```jsx
const EditPartModal = ({ part, onSave, onClose }) => {
  const [edited, setEdited] = useState({ ...part });

  return (
    <Modal title={`Edit Part ${part.id}`} onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 block mb-1">
            Title
          </label>
          <input type="text" value={edited.title}
            onChange={(e) => setEdited({...edited, title: e.target.value})}
            className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                       focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 block mb-1">
            Summary
          </label>
          <textarea rows={3} value={edited.summary}
            onChange={(e) => setEdited({...edited, summary: e.target.value})}
            className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                       focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none resize-none" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 block mb-1">
            Topics (one per line)
          </label>
          <textarea rows={4}
            value={(edited.topics || []).join('\n')}
            onChange={(e) =>
              setEdited({...edited, topics: e.target.value.split('\n')})}
            className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                       focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none
                       resize-none font-mono text-sm" />
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Needs Current Affairs
          </label>
          <ToggleSwitch value={edited.needsCurrentAffairs}
            onChange={(v) => setEdited({...edited, needsCurrentAffairs: v})} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-3 pt-4 border-t">
          <button onClick={onClose}
            className="flex-1 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 py-2
                       rounded-md font-medium hover:bg-gray-50">
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => onSave(edited)}
            className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 text-white py-2 rounded-md
                       font-medium hover:bg-blue-700">
            Save Changes
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};
```

## SCREEN 5 — BLUEPRINT CARDS

### AI Call — Generate Blueprint Per Part

```javascript
const buildBlueprintPrompt = (part, analysisData, settings, features) => `
Create a detailed teaching blueprint for this part of study notes.

Part: ${part.title}
Summary: ${part.summary}
Topics to cover: ${JSON.stringify(part.topics)}
Subject: ${analysisData.subject}
Content type: ${analysisData.contentType}
Enabled features: ${JSON.stringify(Object.entries(features).filter(([,v]) => v).map(([k]) => k))}
Settings: ${JSON.stringify(settings)}

Return JSON:
{
  "headings": {
    "main": "...",
    "subHeadings": ["H2 heading 1", "H2 heading 2", ...]
  },
  "textPlan": {
    "fromSource": ["bullet: content from material to use"],
    "willAdd": ["bullet: content AI will generate"],
    "containers": [
      {"type": "concept_box", "heading": "...", "topic": "..."},
      {"type": "formula_box", "formula": "...", "topic": "..."}
    ]
  },
  "imagePlan": [
    {
      "title": "...",
      "purpose": "...",
      "recommendedStyles": ["infographic", "comparison_matrix", "flowchart"],
      "dataToShow": ["label1", "label2"],
      "isMixedStyle": true,
      "prompt": "Create an educational infographic showing..."
    }
  ],
  "caPlan": {
    "needed": true,
    "searches": [
      {"scope": "national_recent", "query": "..."},
      {"scope": "state_recent", "query": "..."}
    ]
  },
  "validationPlan": [
    "Check source coverage",
    "Verify all formulas are correct",
    "Confirm image relevance"
  ],
  "estimatedLength": "medium",
  "generationPromptPreview": "Write detailed notes about..."
}

Return ONLY valid JSON.
`;
```

```javascript
// Generate blueprints for all parts in parallel
const generateAllBlueprints = async () => {
  const promises = parts.map(part =>
    geminiGenerateText(buildBlueprintPrompt(part, analysisData, settings, features))
      .then(raw => {
        const json = raw.replace(/```json\n?/g, '').replace(/```\n?/g, '').trim();
        return [part.id, JSON.parse(json)];
      })
      .catch(e => [part.id, { error: e.message }])
  );
  const results = await Promise.all(promises);
  const blueprintMap = Object.fromEntries(results);
  setBlueprints(blueprintMap);
};
```

### Blueprint Card Grid

```jsx
const Screen5Blueprints = ({ parts, blueprints, approvals,
                              onApprove, onApproveAll,
                              onViewDetail, onContinue }) => {
  const approvedCount = Object.values(approvals).filter(Boolean).length;
  const allApproved = approvedCount === parts.length;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 py-8">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">
            Teaching Plan (Blueprint)
          </h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500 mt-1">
            Review what I'll cover in each part. Approve to continue.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <span className="text-sm text-gray-600">
            {approvedCount} of {parts.length} approved
          </span>
          <button onClick={onApproveAll}
            className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-4 py-2
                       rounded-md text-sm font-medium">
            ✅ Approve All
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress bar */}
      <div className="h-2 bg-gray-200 rounded-full mb-8 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="h-full bg-green-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
          style={{ width: `${(approvedCount / parts.length) * 100}%` }} />
      </div>

      {/* Blueprint cards grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-8">
        {parts.map(part => {
          const bp = blueprints[part.id];
          const isApproved = approvals[part.id];

          return (
            <div key={part.id}
              className={`bg-white border rounded-lg p-5 shadow-sm
                          transition-all
                ${isApproved ? 'border-green-300 bg-green-50' : 'border-gray-200'}`}>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900">{part.title}</h3>
                {isApproved && (
                  <span className="text-green-600 text-sm font-medium">
                    ✅ Approved
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              {bp && !bp.error ? (
                <>
                  {/* Text plan preview */}
                  <ul className="text-sm text-gray-600 space-y-1 mb-4">
                    {(bp.textPlan?.fromSource || []).slice(0, 3).map((item, i) => (
                      <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                        <span className="text-gray-400 mt-0.5">•</span>
                        <span className="line-clamp-1">{item}</span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>

                  {/* Summary badges */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-4">
                    <span className="text-xs bg-purple-100 text-purple-700
                                     px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      🖼️ {bp.imagePlan?.length || 0} images
                    </span>
                    {bp.caPlan?.needed && (
                      <span className="text-xs bg-amber-100 text-amber-700
                                       px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                        📰 {bp.caPlan.searches?.length || 0} CA searches
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span className="text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700
                                     px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      📦 {bp.textPlan?.containers?.length || 0} containers
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-4">
                  {bp?.error ? `Error: ${bp.error}` : 'Generating blueprint...'}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-gray-100">
                <button onClick={() => onViewDetail(part.id)}
                  className="flex-1 text-sm text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800
                             font-medium text-center py-1">
                  View Full Blueprint
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => onApprove(part.id)}
                  disabled={!bp || bp.error}
                  className={`flex-1 text-sm font-medium py-1 rounded
                    ${isApproved
                      ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700 border border-green-300'
                      : 'bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50'}`}>
                  {isApproved ? '✓ Approved' : 'Approve'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Continue button */}
      <div className="flex justify-end">
        {!allApproved && (
          <p className="text-sm text-amber-600 mr-4 self-center">
            ⚠️ Approve all blueprints to continue
          </p>
        )}
        <button onClick={onContinue} disabled={!allApproved}
          className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-6 py-3
                     rounded-md font-semibold disabled:opacity-40
                     disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
          Continue to Generation →
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

## SCREEN 6 — BLUEPRINT DETAIL (Full Overlay)

```jsx
const Screen6BlueprintDetail = ({ partId, parts, blueprints,
                                   onSave, onApprove, onBack }) => {
  const part = parts.find(p => p.id === partId);
  const blueprint = blueprints[partId];
  const [localBP, setLocalBP] = useState({ ...blueprint });
  const [chatMessages, setChatMessages] = useState([]);
  const [chatInput, setChatInput] = useState('');
  const [chatLoading, setChatLoading] = useState(false);
  const [chatMinimized, setChatMinimized] = useState(false);

  const handleChatSend = async () => {
    if (!chatInput.trim()) return;
    const userMsg = chatInput.trim();
    setChatInput('');
    setChatMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'user', text: userMsg }]);
    setChatLoading(true);

    try {
      const prompt = `
You are helping modify a teaching blueprint for: "${part.title}"
Current blueprint: ${JSON.stringify(localBP)}

User request: "${userMsg}"

Apply the user's request and return the COMPLETE updated blueprint JSON.
Make specific, meaningful changes based on the request.
Return ONLY valid JSON matching the original blueprint structure.
      `;
      const raw = await geminiGenerateText(prompt);
      const json = raw.replace(/```json\n?/g, '').replace(/```\n?/g, '').trim();
      const updated = JSON.parse(json);
      setLocalBP(updated);
      setChatMessages(prev => [...prev, {
        role: 'ai',
        text: `Blueprint updated based on your request: "${userMsg}". Changes applied.`
      }]);
    } catch (e) {
      setChatMessages(prev => [...prev, {
        role: 'ai',
        text: `Could not apply change: ${e.message}. Please try rephrasing.`
      }]);
    } finally {
      setChatLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white z-50 flex flex-col">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="border-b border-gray-200 px-6 py-4 flex items-center
                      justify-between bg-white shadow-sm">
        <button onClick={onBack}
          className="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
          ← Back to Blueprints
        </button>
        <h2 className="font-bold text-gray-900">{part?.title}</h2>
        <button onClick={() => { onSave(partId, localBP); onApprove(partId); }}
          className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-4 py-2
                     rounded-md text-sm font-medium">
          ✅ Save & Approve
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Body */}
      <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
        {/* Left — Scrollable content */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-8 py-6 space-y-8">

          {/* Section 1: Headings */}
          <section>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">
              📑 Headings Plan
            </h3>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <input type="text"
                value={localBP.headings?.main || ''}
                onChange={(e) => setLocalBP({...localBP,
                  headings: {...localBP.headings, main: e.target.value}})}
                placeholder="Main heading (H1)"
                className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                           font-semibold text-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none" />
              {(localBP.headings?.subHeadings || []).map((sh, i) => (
                <input key={i} type="text" value={sh}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const updated = [...(localBP.headings?.subHeadings || [])];
                    updated[i] = e.target.value;
                    setLocalBP({...localBP,
                      headings: {...localBP.headings, subHeadings: updated}});
                  }}
                  placeholder={`Sub-heading ${i + 1}`}
                  className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                             text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none
                             ml-4" />
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* Section 2: Text Plan */}
          <section>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">
              📝 Text Plan
            </h3>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Generation prompt */}
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">
                    Generation Prompt
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(
                        localBP.generationPromptPreview || ''
                      )}
                      className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800">
                      📋 Copy
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={async () => {
                        const regen = await geminiGenerateText(
                          `Generate a detailed text generation prompt for notes about: ${part.title}. Return only the prompt text.`
                        );
                        setLocalBP({...localBP, generationPromptPreview: regen});
                      }}
                      className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800">
                      🔄 Regenerate
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <textarea rows={5}
                  value={localBP.generationPromptPreview || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setLocalBP({...localBP,
                    generationPromptPreview: e.target.value})}
                  className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                             text-sm font-mono focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500
                             outline-none resize-none bg-gray-50" />
              </div>

              {/* Containers planned */}
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 block mb-2">
                  Containers Planned
                </label>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {(localBP.textPlan?.containers || []).map((c, i) => (
                    <div key={i}
                      className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-gray-50 border
                                 border-gray-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2">
                      <span className="text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700
                                       px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-medium">
                        {c.type.replace('_', ' ')}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-sm text-gray-700 flex-1">
                        {c.heading || c.formula || c.topic}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* Section 3: Image Plan */}
          <section>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">
              🖼️ Image Plan
            </h3>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {(localBP.imagePlan || []).map((img, i) => (
                <div key={i}
                  className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-4 bg-gray-50">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                    <h4 className="font-semibold text-gray-800">
                      Image {i + 1}: {img.title}
                    </h4>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-3">
                    {img.purpose}
                  </p>

                  {/* Recommended styles */}
                  <div className="mb-3">
                    <label className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium block mb-1">
                      Recommended Styles
                    </label>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      {(img.recommendedStyles || []).map(style => (
                        <span key={style}
                          className="text-xs bg-purple-100 text-purple-700
                                     px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium">
                          {style}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Style selector */}
                  <div className="mb-3">
                    <label className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium block mb-1">
                      Change Style
                    </label>
                    <select
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const updated = [...localBP.imagePlan];
                        updated[i] = {...img,
                          recommendedStyles: [e.target.value]};
                        setLocalBP({...localBP, imagePlan: updated});
                      }}
                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5
                                 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none">
                      <option>Select style...</option>
                      {VISUAL_STYLES.map(s => (
                        <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  {/* Image prompt */}
                  <div>
                    <label className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium block mb-1">
                      Image Prompt
                    </label>
                    <textarea rows={3} value={img.prompt || ''}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const updated = [...localBP.imagePlan];
                        updated[i] = {...img, prompt: e.target.value};
                        setLocalBP({...localBP, imagePlan: updated});
                      }}
                      className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                                 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none
                                 resize-none" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* Section 4: CA Plan */}
          <section>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">
              📰 Current Affairs Plan
            </h3>
            {localBP.caPlan?.needed ? (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {(localBP.caPlan?.searches || []).map((search, i) => (
                  <div key={i}
                    className="border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 rounded-lg p-3">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-700 uppercase
                                     block mb-1">
                      {search.scope.replace('_', ' ')}
                    </span>
                    <input type="text" value={search.query}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const updated = [...localBP.caPlan.searches];
                        updated[i] = {...search, query: e.target.value};
                        setLocalBP({...localBP,
                          caPlan: {...localBP.caPlan, searches: updated}});
                      }}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-amber-200 rounded
                                 px-2 py-1 text-sm focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400
                                 outline-none" />
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-center py-6 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border
                              border-gray-200">
                <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mb-3">
                  Current affairs not required for this part
                </p>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setLocalBP({...localBP,
                    caPlan: { needed: true,
                      searches: [{ scope: 'national_recent', query: part.title }]
                    }})}
                  className="text-sm text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium">
                  Force Enable Current Affairs
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          {/* Section 5: Validation Plan */}
          <section>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">
              🔍 Validation Plan
            </h3>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {(localBP.validationPlan || []).map((item, i) => (
                <label key={i}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-gray-50 border
                             border-gray-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer
                             hover:bg-gray-100">
                  <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked
                    className="text-blue-600" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-gray-700">{item}</span>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>

        {/* Right — Sticky AI Chat */}
        <div className={`border-l border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 flex flex-col
                         transition-all duration-300
                         ${chatMinimized ? 'w-14' : 'w-80'}`}>
          {chatMinimized ? (
            <button onClick={() => setChatMinimized(false)}
              className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center
                         text-blue-600 hover:bg-gray-100">
              💬
            </button>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3
                              border-b border-gray-200 bg-white">
                <span className="font-semibold text-gray-800 text-sm">
                  💬 AI Assistant
                </span>
                <button onClick={() => setChatMinimized(true)}
                  className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-xs">
                  Minimize ▼
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Chat messages */}
              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-3">
                {chatMessages.length === 0 && (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Try asking:</p>
                    {['Add more images', 'Remove current affairs',
                      'Add formula operations', 'Use comic style',
                      'Make beginner-friendly'].map(q => (
                      <button key={q}
                        onClick={() => {
                          setChatInput(q);
                        }}
                        className="block w-full text-left text-xs bg-white border
                                   border-gray-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                                   hover:bg-blue-50 hover:border-blue-200
                                   text-gray-600">
                        {q}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
                {chatMessages.map((msg, i) => (
                  <div key={i}
                    className={`rounded-lg p-3 text-sm
                      ${msg.role === 'user'
                        ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white ml-4'
                        : 'bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-700'}`}>
                    {msg.text}
                  </div>
                ))}
                {chatLoading && (
                  <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg
                                  p-3 text-sm text-gray-500">
                    ⏳ Updating blueprint...
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Chat input */}
              <div className="p-3 border-t border-gray-200 bg-white">
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input type="text" value={chatInput}
                    onChange={(e) => setChatInput(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleChatSend()}
                    placeholder="Ask to modify..."
                    className="flex-1 border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                               text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none" />
                  <button onClick={handleChatSend} disabled={chatLoading}
                    className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-3 py-2
                               rounded-md text-sm disabled:opacity-50">
                    ↵
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

## SCREEN 7 — GENERATION WORKSPACE

### Layout Structure

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ STICKY TOP BAR                                                  │
│ [Generate All] [▓▓▓░░░ 2/6] [33%] [🔍 Search] [📤 Export]      │
├──────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ SIDEBAR      │ MAIN SCROLL AREA                                 │
│ 200px fixed  │                                                  │
│              │ ═══ Part 1: [Title] ══════════════════════════   │
│ Part 1 ✅    │ [Generate] [Approve Text ☐] [Approve Images ☐]  │
│ Part 2 🔄    │ [Approve Part] [Version] [Validate] [Refine]     │
│ Part 3 ⏳    │ ───────────────────────────────────────────────  │
│ Part 4 ⏳    │ [Generated content: containers, images, CA]      │
│ Part 5 ⏳    │                                                  │

│              │ ...                                              │

└──────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
