### AI Call — Generate Part Notes

```javascript
const buildGenerationPrompt = (part, blueprint, analysisData, settings, features) => `
You are an expert educator writing structured study notes.

Part: ${part.title}
Topics: ${JSON.stringify(part.topics)}
Subject: ${analysisData.subject}
Content type: ${analysisData.contentType}
Difficulty: ${settings.difficulty}
Length: ${settings.length}
Language: ${settings.language}
State focus: ${settings.state || 'none'}
Enabled features: ${JSON.stringify(Object.entries(features).filter(([,v]) => v).map(([k]) => k))}
Blueprint containers planned: ${JSON.stringify(blueprint.textPlan?.containers || [])}

Write comprehensive, well-structured study notes covering all topics.
Format your response as JSON:
{
  "sections": [
    {
      "containerType": "concept_box|formula_box|story_box|comparison_table|code_block|definition_box|pyq_box|quick_revision|geography_box|personality_card|data_insight|analogy_box|timeline_box|process_box|institution_box|ca_box|experiment_box|summary_box",
      "heading": "...",
      "content": "...rich markdown content...",
      "sourceTag": "source_backed|ai_enriched|ca_integrated",
      "formula": "...(if formula_box)",
      "variables": ["..."],
      "operations": ["..."],
      "facts": ["..."],
      "code": "...(if code_block)",
      "language": "python|javascript|etc",
      "inputExample": "...",
      "outputExample": "...",
      "tableData": {"headers": [], "rows": [[]]},
      "steps": ["..."],
      "person": "...",
      "role": "..."
    }
  ],
  "quickRevision": ["key point 1", "key point 2", ...],
  "caNeeded": true|false,
  "caContext": "search query for CA"
}

Make notes comprehensive, educationally rich, and well-structured.
Include real facts and accurate information.
Return ONLY valid JSON.
`;
```

### Content Renderer — All Container Types

```jsx
const ContentSection = ({ section }) => {
  const containers = {
    concept_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border-l-4 border-blue-500 bg-blue-50 p-4 rounded-r-lg my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>💡</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-blue-800">{s.heading}</h3>
          <SourceTag type={s.sourceTag} />
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    formula_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span>🔢</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-indigo-800">{s.heading || 'Formula'}</h3>
        </div>
        {s.formula && (
          <div className="bg-white border border-indigo-300 rounded p-3
                          font-mono text-lg text-center mb-3 text-indigo-900">
            {s.formula}
          </div>
        )}
        {s.content && <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />}
        {(s.variables || []).length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-2">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700 mb-1">Variables:</p>
            <ul className="list-disc pl-4 text-sm space-y-0.5">
              {s.variables.map((v, i) => <li key={i}>{v}</li>)}
            </ul>
          </div>
        )}
        {(s.facts || []).length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-3 bg-indigo-100 rounded p-2">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700 mb-1">Key Facts:</p>
            {s.facts.map((f, i) => (
              <p key={i} className="text-sm flex items-start gap-1">
                <span>→</span><span>{f}</span>
              </p>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        {(s.operations || []).length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-3">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700 mb-1">
              Operations:
            </p>
            {s.operations.map((op, i) => (
              <div key={i}
                className="bg-indigo-100 rounded p-2 text-sm font-mono mt-1">
                {op}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    ),

    code_block: (s) => (
      <div className="rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-300 my-4">
        <div className="bg-gray-800 text-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm
                        flex justify-between items-center">
          <span>{s.language || 'code'}</span>
          <button onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(s.code || '')}
            className="text-xs hover:text-white">📋 Copy</button>
        </div>
        <pre className="bg-gray-900 text-green-400 p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm
                        leading-relaxed">
          <code>{s.code}</code>
        </pre>
        {s.inputExample && (
          <div className="bg-gray-100 border-t border-gray-300 p-3">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-1">INPUT:</p>
            <code className="text-sm text-gray-800">{s.inputExample}</code>
          </div>
        )}
        {s.outputExample && (
          <div className="bg-green-50 border-t border-gray-300 p-3">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-green-700 mb-1">OUTPUT:</p>
            <code className="text-sm text-green-800">{s.outputExample}</code>
          </div>
        )}
        {s.content && (
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-200 bg-white">
            <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    ),

    comparison_table: (s) => (
      <div className="my-4 overflow-x-auto">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>📊</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{s.heading}</h3>
          <SourceTag type={s.sourceTag} />
        </div>
        {s.tableData ? (
          <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                {s.tableData.headers.map((h, i) => (
                  <th key={i}
                    className="border border-gray-300 bg-gray-100 px-4 py-2
                               text-left font-semibold">
                    {h}
                  </th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {s.tableData.rows.map((row, i) => (
                <tr key={i} className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                  {row.map((cell, j) => (
                    <td key={j}
                      className="border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2">
                      {cell}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        ) : (
          <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
        )}
      </div>
    ),

    story_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>📖</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-amber-800">{s.heading}</h3>
          <SourceTag type={s.sourceTag} />
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    geography_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-green-200 bg-green-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>🗺️</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-green-800">{s.heading}</h3>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    personality_card: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-purple-200 bg-purple-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-purple-200 rounded-full flex items-center
                          justify-center text-2xl">👤</div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-purple-800">{s.person || s.heading}</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-purple-600">{s.role || ''}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    pyq_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border-2 border-red-200 bg-red-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>❓</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-red-800">Previous Year Question</h3>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    quick_revision: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-green-300 bg-green-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span>⚡</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-green-800">Quick Revision</h3>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    definition_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border-l-4 border-gray-400 bg-gray-50 p-4 my-4 rounded-r">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
          <span>📌</span>
          <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-700">{s.heading}</h4>
          <SourceTag type={s.sourceTag} />
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    analogy_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>🔗</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-sky-800">{s.heading}</h3>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    timeline_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span>📅</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-violet-800">{s.heading}</h3>
        </div>
        {(s.steps || []).length > 0 ? (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {s.steps.map((step, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex gap-3 items-start">
                <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-violet-500 text-white
                                flex items-center justify-center text-xs
                                flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                  {i + 1}
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-violet-800">{step}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
        )}
      </div>
    ),

    process_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-orange-200 bg-orange-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>⚙️</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-orange-800">{s.heading}</h3>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    institution_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>🏛️</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800">{s.heading}</h3>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    experiment_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50 rounded-lg p-4 my-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span>🔬</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-teal-800">{s.heading}</h3>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),

    summary_box: (s) => (
      <div className="border-2 border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg p-5 my-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span>📋</span>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-blue-800">{s.heading || 'Summary'}</h3>
        </div>
        <MarkdownContent content={s.content} />
      </div>
    ),
  };

  const renderer = containers[section.containerType] || containers.concept_box;
  return renderer(section);
};
```

### Image Card with Real Generation

```jsx
const ImageCard = ({ imagePlan, partId, imageIndex,
                     onApprove, approvedImages }) => {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('idle'); // idle|generating|done|error
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const isApproved = approvedImages?.[`${partId}-${imageIndex}`];

  const generateImage = async () => {
    setStatus('generating');
    setError(null);
    try {
      const prompt = `
        ${imagePlan.prompt}
        Educational study material image.
        Style: ${(imagePlan.recommendedStyles || []).join(' + ')}.
        Include clear labels, legends, and annotations.
        Clean, professional, high-contrast colors.
        No text errors. Accurate educational content.
      `;
      const url = await geminiImageCreation(prompt);
      setImageUrl(url);
      setStatus('done');
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message);
      setStatus('error');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`border rounded-lg overflow-hidden my-4
      ${isApproved ? 'border-green-300' : 'border-gray-200'}`}>
      {/* Image display */}
      <div className="bg-gray-100 min-h-48 flex items-center justify-center">
        {status === 'idle' && (
          <div className="text-center p-6">
            <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mb-3">🖼️ {imagePlan.title}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mb-4">{imagePlan.purpose}</p>
            <button onClick={generateImage}
              className="bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white px-4 py-2
                         rounded-md text-sm font-medium">
              Generate Image
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
        {status === 'generating' && (
          <div className="text-center p-6">
            <div className="animate-spin text-4xl mb-3">⚙️</div>
            <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm">Generating image...</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
              This may take 15–30 seconds
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
        {status === 'done' && imageUrl && (
          <img src={imageUrl} alt={imagePlan.title}
            className="w-full h-auto max-h-80 object-contain" />
        )}
        {status === 'error' && (
          <div className="text-center p-6">
            <p className="text-red-500 text-sm mb-2">⚠️ {error}</p>
            <button onClick={generateImage}
              className="text-sm text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium">
              Try Again
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Controls */}
      <div className="bg-white border-t border-gray-200 px-4 py-3">
        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">
          {imagePlan.title} ·{' '}
          {(imagePlan.recommendedStyles || []).join(', ')}
        </p>
        <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
          {status === 'done' && (
            <>
              <button onClick={generateImage}
                className="text-xs border border-gray-300 text-gray-600
                           hover:bg-gray-50 px-3 py-1 rounded">
                🔄 Regenerate
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => onApprove(`${partId}-${imageIndex}`)}
                className={`text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded font-medium
                  ${isApproved
                    ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700 border border-green-300'
                    : 'bg-green-600 text-white hover:bg-green-700'}`}>
                {isApproved ? '✓ Approved' : '✅ Approve Image'}
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

### Current Affairs Zone

```jsx
const CurrentAffairsZone = ({ caContext, partTitle, settings }) => {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [caResults, setCaResults] = useState({});
  const [loadingScope, setLoadingScope] = useState(null);

  const scopes = [
    { id: 'national_recent', label: '📰 National Recent (2024–today)',
      query: `${caContext} India 2024 current affairs news` },
    { id: 'national_major', label: '📰 National Major (2020–2023)',
      query: `${caContext} India 2020 2021 2022 2023 important events` },
    ...(settings.state ? [
      { id: 'state_recent', label: `📰 ${settings.state} Recent (2024–today)`,
        query: `${caContext} ${settings.state} 2024 current affairs` },
      { id: 'state_major', label: `📰 ${settings.state} Major (2020–2023)`,
        query: `${caContext} ${settings.state} 2020 2023 important` }
    ] : [])
  ];

  const loadCA = async (scope) => {
    setLoadingScope(scope.id);
    try {
      const result = await geminiSearchText(scope.query);
      setCaResults(prev => ({ ...prev, [scope.id]: result }));
    } catch (e) {
      setCaResults(prev => ({ ...prev, [scope.id]: `Error: ${e.message}` }));
    } finally {
      setLoadingScope(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="border border-amber-200 rounded-lg my-4">
      <button onClick={() => setExpanded(!expanded)}
        className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3
                   bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 rounded-t-lg transition-colors">
        <span className="font-medium text-amber-800">
          🔍 Current Affairs for "{partTitle}"
        </span>
        <span className="text-amber-600">{expanded ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>
      </button>

      {expanded && (
        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
          {scopes.map(scope => (
            <div key={scope.id}
              className="border border-amber-100 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-2
                              bg-amber-50">
                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-amber-700">
                  {scope.label}
                </span>
                {!caResults[scope.id] && (
                  <button onClick={() => loadCA(scope)}
                    disabled={loadingScope === scope.id}
                    className="text-xs bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white
                               px-3 py-1 rounded disabled:opacity-50">
                    {loadingScope === scope.id ? '⏳ Searching...' : 'Search'}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
              {caResults[scope.id] && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-white">
                  <MarkdownContent content={caResults[scope.id]} />
                  <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-gray-100">
                    <button className="text-xs bg-green-600 text-white
                                       hover:bg-green-700 px-3 py-1 rounded">
                      ➕ Merge into Notes
                    </button>
                    <button className="text-xs border border-gray-300 text-gray-600
                                       hover:bg-gray-50 px-3 py-1 rounded">
                      🚫 Ignore
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### Visualize This Feature

```jsx
// Add selection listener when generation workspace mounts
useEffect(() => {
  const handleMouseUp = () => {
    const sel = window.getSelection();
    const text = sel?.toString().trim();
    if (text && text.length > 15) {
      const range = sel.getRangeAt(0);
      const rect = range.getBoundingClientRect();
      setVisualizeSelection({
        text,
        x: rect.left + rect.width / 2,
        y: rect.top + window.scrollY - 50
      });
    } else {
      setVisualizeSelection(null);
    }
  };
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
  return () => document.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
}, []);

// Floating button
{visualizeSelection && (
  <button
    style={{
      position: 'absolute',
      left: visualizeSelection.x - 70,
      top: visualizeSelection.y,
      zIndex: 100
    }}
    onClick={() => {
      setVisualizePopupText(visualizeSelection.text);
      setShowVisualizePopup(true);
      setVisualizeSelection(null);
    }}
    className="bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white text-xs
               px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full shadow-lg font-medium">
    🎨 Visualize This
  </button>
)}
```

```jsx
const VisualizePopup = ({ selectedText, onClose, onInsert }) => {
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('');
  const [selectedStyles, setSelectedStyles] = useState([]);
  const [isMixed, setIsMixed] = useState(false);
  const [generating, setGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState(null);

  // Auto-generate prompt from selected text
  useEffect(() => {
    const autoPrompt = async () => {
      try {
        const p = await geminiGenerateText(
          `Create a detailed educational image prompt for this concept: "${selectedText}". Return only the image prompt, 2-3 sentences.`
        );
        setPrompt(p);
      } catch (e) {
        setPrompt(`Create an educational visual explaining: ${selectedText}`);
      }
    };
    autoPrompt();
  }, [selectedText]);

  const generate = async () => {
    setGenerating(true);
    try {
      const styleDesc = isMixed
        ? `Mixed visual combining: ${selectedStyles.join(', ')}`
        : `Style: ${selectedStyles[0] || 'infographic'}`;
      const finalPrompt = `${prompt}. ${styleDesc}. Educational, clear labels, professional.`;
      const url = await geminiImageCreation(finalPrompt);
      setImageUrl(url);
    } catch (e) {
      alert('Image generation failed: ' + e.message);
    } finally {
      setGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal title="🎨 Visualize This" onClose={onClose} wide>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-lg p-3">
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-1">Selected text:</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-800 italic">"{selectedText}"</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 block mb-1">
            Image Prompt
          </label>
          <textarea rows={4} value={prompt}
            onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                       text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none resize-none" />
        </div>

        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Visual Style
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-600">
              <ToggleSwitch value={isMixed}
                onChange={setIsMixed} />
              Mixed styles
            </label>
          </div>

          {/* Style dropdown or multi-select */}
          {isMixed ? (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {VISUAL_STYLES.slice(0, 12).map(s => (
                <button key={s.id}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedStyles(prev =>
                    prev.includes(s.id)
                      ? prev.filter(x => x !== s.id)
                      : [...prev, s.id]
                  )}
                  className={`text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-full border font-medium
                    ${selectedStyles.includes(s.id)
                      ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white border-purple-600'
                      : 'bg-white text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:border-purple-400'}`}>
                  {s.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <select
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedStyles([e.target.value])}
              className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm
                         focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none">
              <option>Select style...</option>
              {VISUAL_STYLES.map(s => (
                <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}
        </div>

        <button onClick={generate}
          disabled={generating || (!isMixed && !selectedStyles[0])}
          className="w-full bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white py-2
                     rounded-md font-medium disabled:opacity-50">
          {generating ? '⏳ Generating...' : '🖼️ Generate Image'}
        </button>

        {imageUrl && (
          <div>
            <img src={imageUrl} alt="Generated visual"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
            <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
              <button onClick={() => onInsert(imageUrl)}
                className="flex-1 bg-green-600 text-white py-2 rounded-md
                           text-sm font-medium hover:bg-green-700">
                Insert Here
              </button>
              <button onClick={generate}
                className="flex-1 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 py-2
                           rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-gray-50">
                Regenerate
              </button>
              <button onClick={onClose}
                className="border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 py-2 px-4
                           rounded-md text-sm hover:bg-gray-50">
                Discard
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};
```

### AI Validation (Gap #9 — Full Workflow)

```javascript
const runValidation = async (partContent, blueprint, part) => {
  const prompt = `
You are an expert educational content validator.

Review these study notes for quality, accuracy, and completeness.
Part title: ${part.title}
Blueprint planned: ${JSON.stringify(blueprint?.validationPlan || [])}

Notes content summary:
${JSON.stringify(partContent?.sections?.map(s =>
  ({ type: s.containerType, heading: s.heading })) || []
)}

Return JSON:
{
  "overallScore": 82,
  "grade": "Good",
  "strengths": ["point 1", "point 2"],
  "improvements": [
    {
      "priority": "high|medium|low",
      "issue": "...",
      "suggestion": "...",
      "autoFixable": false
    }
  ],
  "missingElements": ["element 1"],
  "accuracyNotes": ["note 1"]
}

Return ONLY valid JSON.
  `;

  const raw = await geminiGenerateText(prompt);
  const json = raw.replace(/```json\n?/g, '').replace(/```\n?/g, '').trim();
  return JSON.parse(json);
};
```

### Granular Approval System (Gap #8)

```jsx
const PartApprovalBar = ({ partId, approval, onApproveText,
                            onApproveImages, onApprovePart,
                            onUnlock, generatedContent }) => {
  const textApproved = approval?.textApproved;
  const imagesApproved = approval?.imagesApproved;
  const partApproved = approval?.partApproved;
  const canApprovePart = textApproved && imagesApproved;

  return (
    <div className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg
      ${partApproved ? 'bg-green-50 border border-green-300'
                     : 'bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200'}`}>

      {partApproved ? (
        <>
          <span className="text-green-700 font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            ✅ Part Approved
          </span>
          <button onClick={() => onUnlock(partId)}
            className="text-sm text-amber-600 hover:text-amber-800 font-medium
                       border border-amber-300 px-3 py-1 rounded">
            🔓 Unlock to Edit
          </button>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <button onClick={() => onApproveText(partId)}
            className={`text-sm px-3 py-1.5 rounded font-medium
              ${textApproved
                ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700 border border-green-300'
                : 'bg-white border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}>
            {textApproved ? '✓ Text Approved' : 'Approve Text ☐'}
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => onApproveImages(partId)}
            className={`text-sm px-3 py-1.5 rounded font-medium
              ${imagesApproved
                ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700 border border-green-300'
                : 'bg-white border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}>
            {imagesApproved ? '✓ Images Approved' : 'Approve Images ☐'}
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => onApprovePart(partId)}
            disabled={!canApprovePart}
            title={!canApprovePart
              ? 'Approve text and images first' : ''}
            className="text-sm bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white
                       px-4 py-1.5 rounded font-medium disabled:opacity-40
                       disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
            ✅ Approve Part
          </button>

          {!canApprovePart && (
            <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">
              {!textApproved && !imagesApproved
                ? 'Approve text + images first'
                : !textApproved ? 'Approve text first'
                : 'Approve images first'}
            </span>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

## SCREEN 8 — EXPORT

```jsx
const Screen8Export = ({ parts, generated, approvals, analysisData }) => {
  const [format, setFormat] = useState('html');
  const [options, setOptions] = useState({
    coverPage: true, toc: true, images: true,
    ca: true, pyqs: true, sourceTags: true,
    blueprintPrompts: false
  });

  const approvedCount = parts.filter(p => approvals[p.id]?.partApproved).length;

  const buildExportHTML = () => {
    // Compile all approved parts into clean HTML document
    const sections = parts.map(part => {
      const content = generated[part.id];
      if (!content || !approvals[part.id]?.partApproved) return '';

      const sectionHtml = (content.sections || []).map(s => {
        return `<div class="content-section container-${s.containerType}">
          <h3>${sanitizeOutput(s.heading || '')}</h3>
          <div class="content">${sanitizeOutput(s.content || '')}</div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('\n');

      return `
        <section class="part-section">
          <h2>${sanitizeOutput(part.title)}</h2>
          ${sectionHtml}
        </section>
      `;
    }).join('\n');

    return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<title>StudyForge Notes — ${analysisData.subject}</title>
<style>
  body { font-family: system-ui, sans-serif; max-width: 800px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 2rem; line-height: 1.75; color: #111827; }
  h1, h2, h3 { font-weight: 700; }
  h1 { font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem; }
  h2 { font-size: 1.5rem; margin-top: 3rem; border-bottom: 2px solid #E5E7EB; padding-bottom: 0.5rem; }
  h3 { font-size: 1.2rem; margin-top: 2rem; }
  table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 1.5rem 0; }
  th { background: #F3F4F6; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #E5E7EB; }
  td { padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #E5E7EB; }
  code { background: #F3F4F6; padding: 0.2em 0.4em; border-radius: 4px; }
  pre { background: #1F2937; color: #F9FAFB; padding: 1rem; border-radius: 8px; overflow-x: auto; }
  .container-formula_box { background: #EEF2FF; border: 1px solid #C7D2FE; border-radius: 8px; padding: 1rem; margin: 1rem 0; }
  .container-concept_box { border-left: 4px solid #2563EB; background: #EFF6FF; padding: 1rem; margin: 1rem 0; }
  .container-story_box { background: #FFFBEB; border: 1px solid #FCD34D; border-radius: 8px; padding: 1rem; margin: 1rem 0; }
  @media print { h2 { page-break-before: always; } }
</style>
</head>
<body>
${options.coverPage ? `
  <div style="text-align:center; padding: 4rem 0; border-bottom: 2px solid #E5E7EB; margin-bottom: 3rem;">
    <h1>${analysisData.subject}</h1>
    <p style="color: #6B7280; font-size: 1.1rem;">${analysisData.subDiscipline}</p>
    <p style="color: #9CA3AF;">Generated by StudyForge · ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}</p>
  </div>` : ''}
${sections}
</body>
</html>`;
  };

  const handleDownload = () => {
    if (format === 'html') {
      const html = buildExportHTML();
      const blob = new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `studyforge-${analysisData.subject?.replace(/\s+/g, '-')}.html`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } else if (format === 'print') {
      window.print();
    } else if (format === 'copy') {
      const html = buildExportHTML();
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(html);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 py-8">
      <div className="text-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">
          🎉 {approvedCount} of {parts.length} parts approved!
        </h1>
        <p className="text-gray-500 mt-2">
          Your study notes are ready to export
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* Settings */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">Export Settings</h2>

          {/* Format selection */}
          <div className="mb-6">
            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 block mb-2">
              Format
            </label>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {[
                { id: 'html', label: '📄 Single HTML File' },
                { id: 'print', label: '🖨️ Print / Save as PDF' },
                { id: 'copy', label: '📋 Copy HTML to Clipboard' }
              ].map(f => (
                <label key={f.id}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
                  <input type="radio" name="format" value={f.id}
                    checked={format === f.id}
                    onChange={() => setFormat(f.id)}
                    className="text-blue-600" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-gray-700">{f.label}</span>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Include options */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 block mb-2">
              Include
            </label>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {[
                { id: 'coverPage', label: 'Cover page' },
                { id: 'toc', label: 'Table of contents' },
                { id: 'images', label: 'All generated images' },
                { id: 'ca', label: 'Current affairs sections' },
                { id: 'pyqs', label: 'PYQ sections' },
                { id: 'sourceTags', label: 'Source tags (color labels)' },
                { id: 'blueprintPrompts', label: 'Blueprint prompts (reference)' }
              ].map(opt => (
                <label key={opt.id}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
                  <input type="checkbox"
                    checked={options[opt.id]}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setOptions({...options, [opt.id]: e.target.checked})}
                    className="text-blue-600 rounded" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-gray-700">{opt.label}</span>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Preview */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">Preview</h2>
          <div className="h-80 overflow-y-auto border border-gray-200 rounded-lg
                          p-4 bg-gray-50 text-sm prose-content">
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold">{analysisData.subject}</h1>
            <p className="text-gray-500">{analysisData.subDiscipline}</p>
            <hr className="my-4" />
            {parts.slice(0, 2).map(part => (
              <div key={part.id} className="mb-4">
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold">{part.title}</h2>
                {(generated[part.id]?.sections || []).slice(0, 2).map((s, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="mb-2">
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">{s.heading}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 line-clamp-2">
                      {s.content?.replace(/[#*`]/g, '').substring(0, 120)}...
                    </p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Download button */}
      <div className="flex gap-4 justify-center mt-8">
        <button onClick={handleDownload}
          className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-8 py-3
                     rounded-lg font-bold text-lg shadow-md hover:shadow-lg
                     transition-all">
          ⬇️ Download Notes
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

                     ${wide ? 'max-w-2xl' : 'max-w-lg'} w-full

### Toggle Switch

```jsx
const ToggleSwitch = ({ value, onChange }) => (
  <button
    onClick={() => onChange(!value)}
    className={`relative w-10 h-6 rounded-full transition-colors duration-200
                flex-shrink-0
      ${value ? 'bg-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-300'}`}>
    <span className={`absolute top-1 left-1 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-white
                      shadow transition-transform duration-200
      ${value ? 'translate-x-4' : 'translate-x-0'}`} />
  </button>
);
```

### Source Tag

```jsx
const SourceTag = ({ type }) => {
  const tags = {
    source_backed: { label: 'Source', className: 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' },
    ai_enriched:   { label: 'AI Added', className: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' },
    ca_integrated: { label: 'Current Affairs', className: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700' }
  };
  const tag = tags[type];
  if (!tag) return null;
  return (
    <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium ${tag.className}`}>
      {tag.label}
    </span>
  );
};
```

### Markdown Content Renderer

```jsx
const MarkdownContent = ({ content }) => {
  if (!content) return null;

  const parseMarkdown = (md) => {
    if (typeof md !== 'string') return '';
    return md
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
      .replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, '<em>$1</em>')
      .replace(/`(.+?)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
      .replace(/^### (.+)$/gm, '<h3>$1</h3>')
      .replace(/^## (.+)$/gm, '<h2>$1</h2>')
      .replace(/^# (.+)$/gm, '<h1>$1</h1>')
      .replace(/^\* (.+)$/gm, '<li>$1</li>')
      .replace(/^- (.+)$/gm, '<li>$1</li>')
      .replace(/(<li>.*<\/li>)/s, '<ul>$1</ul>')
      .replace(/\n\n/g, '</p><p>')
      .replace(/^(?!<[hlu])/m, '<p>')
      .replace(/(?<![>])$/m, '</p>');
  };

  return (
    <div className="prose-content text-sm"
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: parseMarkdown(content) }} />
  );
};
```

### Error Modal

```jsx
const ErrorModal = ({ action, errorMessage, onRetry, onSkip, onClose }) => (
  <Modal title="Something went wrong" onClose={onClose}>
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
        <p className="font-semibold text-red-800">Failed to {action}</p>
        <p className="text-sm text-red-600 mt-1">{errorMessage}</p>
      </div>
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">What would you like to do?</p>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <button onClick={onRetry}
          className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-medium">
          Try Again
        </button>
        <button onClick={onSkip}
          className="border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-4 py-2
                     rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-gray-50">
          Skip This Step
        </button>
        <button onClick={onClose}
          className="text-gray-500 px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:text-gray-700">
          Cancel
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </Modal>
);
```

const PromptCenter = ({ prompts, onUpdate, onClose }) => {
  const promptCards = [
    { id: 'analysis', label: 'Material Analysis Prompt', screen: 'Screen 2-3' },
    { id: 'parts', label: 'Part Splitting Prompt', screen: 'Screen 4' },
    { id: 'blueprint', label: 'Blueprint Generation Prompt', screen: 'Screen 5' },
    { id: 'generation', label: 'Notes Generation Prompt', screen: 'Screen 7' },
    { id: 'image', label: 'Image Generation Prompt', screen: 'Screen 7' },
    { id: 'ca', label: 'Current Affairs Search Prompt', screen: 'Screen 7' },
    { id: 'validation', label: 'AI Validation Prompt', screen: 'Screen 7' },
  ];

  return (
    <Modal title="🔧 Prompt Center" onClose={onClose} wide>
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-6">
        View, edit, and copy the AI prompts used at each stage.
        Changes affect all future generations.
      </p>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {promptCards.map(card => (
          <details key={card.id}
            className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
            <summary className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3
                                bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100">
              <div>
                <span className="font-medium text-gray-800">{card.label}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 ml-2">({card.screen})</span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-gray-400 text-xs">▼</span>
            </summary>
            <div className="p-4">
              <textarea rows={8}
                value={prompts[card.id] || ''}
                onChange={(e) => onUpdate(card.id, e.target.value)}
                className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2
                           text-xs font-mono focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500
                           outline-none resize-y bg-gray-50" />
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(prompts[card.id] || '')}
                  className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800">
                  📋 Copy
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </details>
        ))}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

## HTML SANITIZATION — APPLY EVERYWHERE (Gap #12)

```javascript
// Sanitize before inserting into HTML or sending to AI
const sanitizeInput = (str) => {
  if (typeof str !== 'string') return String(str || '');
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
};

// Sanitize output before rendering
const sanitizeOutput = (str) => {
  if (typeof str !== 'string') return '';
  return str.replace(/<script\b[^<]*(?:(?!<\/script>)<[^<]*)*<\/script>/gi, '');
};

// Use sanitizeInput() on ALL user-typed prompt inputs before sending to AI
// Use sanitizeOutput() on ALL AI responses before inserting into innerHTML
```

## QUALITY REQUIREMENTS

```
Performance:
  - Blueprint generation for all parts runs in parallel (Promise.all)
  - Image generation is async — text shows immediately
  - Never block UI thread

Error Handling:
  - Every geminiGenerateText() call in try/catch
  - Every geminiSearchText() call in try/catch
  - Every geminiImageCreation() call in try/catch
  - Show ErrorModal with retry option on all failures
  - Parse JSON safely with .replace() cleanup before JSON.parse()

Code Quality:
  - Comment every major section
  - Use descriptive names
  - Keep components focused and readable

User Experience:
  - Loading spinners on every async action
  - Success messages on saves and approvals
  - No dead buttons — every button does something
  - Smooth CSS transitions between screens (0.3s fade)

Security:
  - sanitizeInput() on all user-typed prompts before AI call
  - sanitizeOutput() on all AI responses before innerHTML
  - No eval(), no innerHTML from unsanitized sources
```

Hi [Sublead Name],

Before you audit any developer, read this document. It contains:
1. Additional functional gaps we may have missed

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION 1 — ADDITIONAL FUNCTIONAL GAPS NOT IN DOC OR PROMPT A
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

GAP 11 — MULTI-LANGUAGE SUPPORT
Doc doesn't mention language handling.
- What if user uploads a Hindi PDF?
- What if content is bilingual (English + regional language)?
- Should output be in same language as input, or user-selectable?
- Should we support Devanagari, Tamil, Telugu, Bengali scripts?
Ask developer: what happens if I upload non-English content?

GAP 12 — LARGE FILE HANDLING
Doc says "500 pages max" but doesn't specify:
- What if file is 501 pages? Hard reject or graceful?
- What if file is 50MB but only 100 pages (images-heavy)?
- Progress indicator for very large files (multi-minute reads)?
- Chunking strategy for AI context limits?
- Memory management in browser (localStorage has ~5MB limit)?

GAP 13 — DUPLICATE / REPEATED CONTENT DETECTION
- What if same formula appears 5 times in source?
- What if same PYQ is repeated?
- What if user uploads same file twice?
- Deduplication logic before generation?

GAP 14 — CONTENT WITH MIXED SUBJECTS
- What if PDF has both Economics AND Geography chapters?
- Should app split by subject and generate separate documents?
- Should app force single subject and warn user?
- How does subject-adaptive UI handle multi-subject?

GAP 15 — HANDLING POOR-QUALITY SOURCE
- What if OCR fails on scanned PDF?
- What if PDF has garbled text (encoding issues)?
- What if paste text has formatting artifacts (bullet chars, weird spaces)?
- Fallback behavior?

GAP 16 — USER-PROVIDED IMAGES IN INPUT
- What if user uploads JPG/PNG only (no text file)?
- OCR strategy for pure-image input?
- What if image has diagrams (flowcharts) not just text?
- Should app extract diagram meaning, not just text?

GAP 17 — VERSIONING & UNDO
Doc doesn't mention:
- Can user undo an approval?
- Can user rollback a regeneration to previous version?
- Version history for each part?
- Compare-versions view?
- "Show what changed" after regeneration?

GAP 18 — COLLABORATIVE FEATURES
Doc treats app as single-user. Should we support:
- Sharing a session via URL?
- Read-only preview link?
- Multi-user editing?
- Comments/annotations by others?
(Probably out of scope but confirm with architect)

GAP 19 — CONTENT SAFETY & MODERATION
- What if source has offensive content?
- What if AI generates inappropriate output?
- Content warning system?
- Filter for exam-inappropriate material?

GAP 20 — CITATION & ATTRIBUTION
Traceability tags exist (SOURCE_BACKED etc.) but doc doesn't specify:
- Page number citation format?
- Bibliographic references for research-required content?
- How to cite AI-generated content in exams?
- Copyright handling for uploaded material?

GAP 21 — SEARCH WITHIN GENERATED NOTES
- Can user search across all 6 parts of generated notes?
- Find & replace functionality?
- Filter by tag (show only SOURCE_BACKED)?
- Filter by container type (show only formulas)?

GAP 22 — BOOKMARK / STAR SYSTEM
- Can user bookmark important sections?
- Star specific formulas/definitions for quick review?
- Generate a "starred items only" export?

GAP 23 — REVISION MODE
- After full generation, can user enter "revision mode"?
- Auto-hide answers, show only questions?
- Flashcard mode from generated content?
- Quiz generation from PYQs and content?

GAP 24 — PRINT OPTIMIZATION
- PDF export via browser print — is it print-optimized?
- Page breaks at right places (not mid-formula)?
- Print-friendly color scheme (or B&W mode)?
- Print preview before download?

GAP 25 — MOBILE / TABLET USE
Doc says "modern browsers" but:
- Is UI responsive for tablet?
- Touch-friendly for mobile?
- Highlight-to-visualize on touchscreens?
- Sidebar collapse on small screens?

GAP 26 — ACCESSIBILITY (A11Y)
Doc doesn't mention:
- Screen reader support (ARIA labels)?
- Keyboard-only navigation?
- Color contrast for visually impaired?
- Alt text for generated images?
- Font size adjustment?

GAP 27 — ANALYTICS & USAGE TRACKING
- Does app log user actions locally?
- Anonymous usage stats for improving product?
- Which features are most used?
- Which parts users regenerate most (indicates poor quality)?

GAP 28 — OFFLINE MODE
- Can app work offline once loaded?
- What if AI call fails due to network?
- Queue actions for when connection returns?

GAP 29 — SESSION MANAGEMENT
- What if user opens app in 2 tabs?
- localStorage conflict handling?
- Session timeout for security?
- Multiple sessions per browser?

GAP 30 — DATA EXPORT/IMPORT
- Can user export the app state as JSON (backup)?
- Import previous session from JSON?
- Migrate between browsers?
- Cloud sync (if supported later)?

GAP 31 — TEMPLATES & PRESETS
- Save current settings as template for reuse?
- "UPSC Prelims preset" that auto-selects relevant bundles?
- "Academic notes preset"?
- Community-shared presets?

GAP 32 — PROMPT LIBRARY
- Save custom prompts as reusable templates?
- Share prompts between parts?
- Prompt version history?
- Rate/improve prompts based on output quality?

GAP 33 — FEEDBACK LOOP
- User rates each generated part (thumbs up/down)?
- User marks specific issues (factual error, poor image, etc.)?
- Feedback fed back to improve future generations?

GAP 34 — HELP & ONBOARDING
- First-time user tutorial?
- Tooltips on every button?
- Contextual help ("What is a blueprint?")?
- Video walkthroughs?
- FAQ section?

GAP 35 — MULTIPLE DOCUMENT COMPARISON
- What if user wants to compare 2 sources?
- Side-by-side upload and unified notes?

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION 2 — NON-FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS NOT COVERED
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

PERFORMANCE:
- Doc says "smooth for 500 pages" but no benchmarks defined
- Target FCP (First Contentful Paint)?
- Target TTI (Time To Interactive)?
- Max acceptable AI call latency?
- Max acceptable image generation time?
- Memory usage cap?
- CPU usage on generation?
Ask developer: measure and report actual numbers.

SECURITY:
- Uploaded files stored where? (browser only? sent to server?)
- If sent to server, encryption in transit?
- User data privacy — what's stored, for how long?
- CSRF protection?
- XSS protection (esp. in generated HTML output)?
- Input sanitization for paste text?
- Safe HTML rendering (no injected scripts)?

RELIABILITY:
- Auto-retry defined (2 retries, 2s + 4s wait) — but:
  - What after 2 failed retries — silent fail or notify?
  - Circular retry loop prevention?
  - Rate limit handling?
- Recovery from browser crash?
- Recovery from localStorage full?

SCALABILITY:
- What if user creates 20 parts (not 6)?
- What if 50 images per part?
- UI performance with 100+ containers on one page?
- Lazy loading strategy for long documents?

MAINTAINABILITY:
- Code modularity (single HTML — but is JS well-organized?)
- Function naming conventions
- Comments for complex logic
- Ability to swap AI providers later
- Ability to add new bundles later
- Ability to add new container types later

USABILITY:
- Consistent button placement across screens
- Consistent color meaning (green = success everywhere?)
- Loading states for every async action
- Empty states (what if no CA found?)
- Error states (what if generation returns nothing?)
- Success confirmations (visual/audio feedback)

COMPATIBILITY:
- Chrome version min?
- Firefox version min?
- Edge version min?
- Safari support?
- Screen resolution min/max?

OBSERVABILITY:
- Console logs for debugging?
- Error reporting mechanism?
- Performance profiling hooks?
- Feature flag system for A/B testing?

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION 3 — EDGE CASES DOC IGNORES
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

INPUT EDGE CASES:
- Empty file uploaded (0 bytes)
- Corrupt PDF
- Password-protected PDF
- File with only images, no text
- File in unsupported language script
- Paste text with 1 million characters
- Topic name with special characters (₹, emoji, HTML tags)
- Sub-topic list with all 10 fields identical

READING EDGE CASES:
- Source content is 3 sentences (too short to split)
- Source content is 2000 pages (over limit)
- Source is entirely tables, no prose
- Source is entirely diagrams (image-heavy)
- Multiple columns in PDF
- Right-to-left text (Urdu, Arabic)

FEATURE DETECTION EDGE CASES:
- Content has NO detectable features (rare abstract essay)
- Content has ALL features simultaneously
- Content forces contradictory features (fiction + formulas)
- Detection confidence too low (< 30%) — hide feature?

BLUEPRINT EDGE CASES:
- User approves blueprint but then edits back to unapproved state
- User approves all blueprints then removes one — reset generation?
- User modifies blueprint after generation started
- Blueprint requires bundles user disabled — warn or override?

GENERATION EDGE CASES:
- AI generates blank response — retry or show empty?
- AI generates broken markdown — sanitize how?
- Image generation fails 3 times — placeholder or skip?
- CA search returns 0 results — hide section or show "none"?
- Validation finds critical error — block approval or warn?

APPROVAL EDGE CASES:
- User approves part then edits it — unapprove automatically?
- User approves all parts then adds new part — new part needs approval?
- Partial approval (text approved, image not) — export handling?

EXPORT EDGE CASES:
- User exports mid-generation (only 3 of 6 parts done)
- Export with all include options unchecked — what happens?
- HTML export with broken images (regenerate placeholders?)
- Download blocked by browser — user notification?

STATE EDGE CASES:
- localStorage quota exceeded during generation
- Multiple tabs open with same session
- User clears cookies mid-session
- Browser private/incognito mode (no persistence)
- Cross-device continuation (not supported? explicit warning?)

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION 4 — ARCHITECTURAL CHECKS SUBLEAD MUST DO
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Before judging any developer's work, verify these:

CHECK A — CODE ARCHITECTURE
Ask developer:
1. Show me your JS module structure
2. Where is state defined?
3. Where are AI calls abstracted?
4. Where is rendering logic?
5. Where is event handling?
6. Is there separation between UI, state, and logic?
7. Or is it all mixed in one file?

Good architecture = separated concerns, clear layers
Bad architecture = spaghetti code, tightly coupled

CHECK B — STATE MANAGEMENT
Ask developer:
1. Show me your state object structure
2. Every state variable — its type and purpose
3. Where is state read from localStorage?
4. Where is state written to localStorage?
5. Any state that lives outside your central state object?
6. Race conditions when async operations update state?

Good = single source of truth, all mutations go through defined functions
Bad = scattered globals, direct DOM edits without state update

CHECK C — EVENT HANDLING & BUTTON MAPPING
Sublead must manually check EVERY button on EVERY screen:
1. Does it have an onclick handler?
2. Which function does it call?
3. Does that function actually exist?
4. Does the function do what the button label promises?
5. Any button that's decorative (fake)?

Create a spreadsheet:
| Screen | Button Label | Handler Function | Actually Works? |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Continue | handleContinue1() | Yes |
| 4 | Edit Settings | showEditModal() | No — only toast |

CHECK D — ROUTING LOGIC
1. Every navigation path — forward and backward
2. Which function moves between screens?
3. Are there guards (must approve before continue)?
4. Can user break flow by clicking sidebar?
5. Deep-linking support?

Draw the state machine on paper:
Screen 1 → Screen 2 (auto, no user action)
Screen 2 → Screen 3 (auto, timer-based)
Screen 3 → Screen 4 (user click "Continue")
etc.

CHECK E — DATA FLOW
Trace one piece of data end-to-end:
- User types "National Income" in Method 2
- Where is it stored?
- How does it get passed to Screen 2?
- How does it become part of the analysis prompt?
- Where does it appear in Screen 3 output?
- Is it in the final export?

If any link in this chain is broken, entire feature is fake.

CHECK F — PROMPT VISIBILITY
Every AI operation should have visible prompt:
1. Count total AI operations in the app (analyze, split, blueprint, 
   text, image, CA, validation, refine)
2. For each, check if there's a "View Prompt" affordance
3. Verify prompt shown matches prompt actually used (not fake display)
4. Verify user can edit and regenerate with edited prompt

CHECK G — CONTAINER RENDERING
For each of the 22 container types:
1. Is CSS defined?
2. Is HTML template ready?
3. Is there a function that renders this container with data?
4. Show me one live example on Screen 7 for each type
5. Any container that's defined but never rendered?

CHECK H — FEATURE BUNDLE INTEGRATION
1. When user toggles a bundle feature on Screen 3, where is it saved?
2. When Screen 5 shows bundle icons per part, where does it read from?
3. When Screen 7 renders bundle containers, which state key drives it?
4. Is there a code path from toggle → save → render → export?

If the chain breaks anywhere, bundles are cosmetic.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION 5 — JUDGMENT PROTOCOL (BEFORE/DURING/AFTER)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

BEFORE JUDGING A DEVELOPER'S WORK:

Step 1 — Get the file
- Save with clear name: [devname]-[phase]-[date].html
- Verify file opens in Chrome, Firefox, Edge
- Note file size (should be < 500KB for skeleton)

Step 2 — Get their algorithm explanation
- Send Prompt B (stage-by-stage audit)
- Wait for full 4-part reply
- Do NOT proceed if reply is prose or marketing

Step 3 — Understand their architecture
- Ask CHECK A questions from Section 4
- Ask them to draw or describe their component tree
- Ask about state management approach
- Note their answers verbatim

DURING JUDGING:

Step 4 — Test the buttons systematically
- Open browser dev tools
- Click every button on every screen
- Note any console errors
- Check network tab (should be empty for skeleton — no API calls)
- Verify each button either:
  a) Opens a real modal/overlay
  b) Updates the UI meaningfully
  c) Persists something to localStorage
  Not just: shows a toast that disappears

Step 5 — Test the flows end-to-end
- Fresh session: complete all 8 screens
- Refresh on Screen 4: verify state restored
- Refresh on Screen 7: verify state restored + all parts still visible
- Click "Start Over": verify complete reset
- Complete session in incognito: verify works without prior state

Step 6 — Test edge cases from Section 3
- Pick 5 edge cases per screen
- Actually try them
- Note behavior: graceful / crash / silent fail

Step 7 — Test the gaps from Section 1
- For each of the 35 additional gaps
- Check if developer addressed
- If not, note whether it's blocking or nice-to-have

Step 8 — Test non-functional requirements from Section 2
- Time the reading phase
- Time each generation
- Measure memory usage in dev tools
- Check for console errors on any action
- Verify keyboard navigation works

AFTER JUDGING:

Step 9 — Score honestly by section
Create scorecard:
| Section | Total | Working | Partial | Broken | Missing |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Screen 1 features | 20 | 15 | 3 | 1 | 1 |
| Screen 2 features | 10 | 10 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| ... | ... | ... | ... | ... | ... |
| Gaps (35) | 35 | 5 | 10 | 5 | 15 |
| Non-functional (7) | 7 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 |
| Architecture | 8 | 6 | 2 | 0 | 0 |

Step 10 — Write neutral report
Format:
- What works well
- What partially works (needs fix)
- What is broken (blocking)
- What is missing (needs to be added)
- Overall grade based on evidence, not developer's claim

Step 11 — DO NOT accept developer's self-assessment
- Ignore any "310/310" claim
- Ignore any "A+" claim  
- Ignore any "Excellent" claim
- Base judgment only on YOUR manual testing

Step 12 — Report to me (lead) with structured summary
| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| Developer | akshatpck |
| Phase tested | Phase 2 |
| Total features tested | 275 |
| Fully working | 180 |
| Partial | 45 |
| Broken | 20 |
| Missing | 30 |
| Overall % | 65% |
| Blocking issues | 3 (list them) |
| Recommendation | Continue / Rebuild / Drop |

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION 6 — RULES FOR SUBLEAD DURING VERIFICATION
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

RULE 1: Never trust self-scoring
Every developer will inflate their score. Assume all claims are 
false until you verify manually.

RULE 2: Manual test > developer explanation
If developer says "it works" but you can't make it work, IT DOESN'T WORK.

RULE 3: One stage at a time
Do not audit multiple stages in one session. Focus fully on Stage N.
Complete audit → sign off → move to Stage N+1.

RULE 4: Ask "show me" not "tell me"
- Don't accept: "yes it handles that edge case"
- Do ask: "show me what happens when I do X"

RULE 5: Trace one feature end-to-end
Pick one feature per session and follow it through code:
- HTML element → event handler → function → state update → 
  localStorage → re-render → UI update
If any link is missing, feature is fake.

RULE 6: When in doubt, ask developer directly
Do NOT assume anything. Do NOT guess.
If unclear: "AkshatGemini, please explain [specific behavior]. 
Show me the code path."

RULE 7: Document everything
Create a shared doc where you log:
- Every test performed
- Every bug found
- Every question raised to developer
- Every answer received
- Every gap identified

RULE 8: Prioritize blocking issues
Not every gap is equal:
- BLOCKING: Users can't complete the flow (must fix immediately)
- MAJOR: Feature exists but broken (fix in next phase)
- MINOR: Nice-to-have missing (fix eventually)
- COSMETIC: Visual polish (fix at end)

RULE 9: Don't rebuild — direct the developer
Your job is verification and direction, not coding.
When you find a bug: write a fix sheet, send it, verify fix.
Do not write patches yourself.

RULE 10: Escalate honestly
If developer becomes uncooperative, defensive, or dishonest:
- Document specific instances (quote their replies)
- Report to me (lead) with evidence
- Do not tolerate marketing responses

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION 7 — QUESTIONS TO ASK DEVELOPER DIRECTLY
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

When in doubt about implementation, ask these directly:

ARCHITECTURE QUESTIONS:
1. Draw your app's architecture on paper. Show me.
2. How do you handle state across the 8 screens?
3. Where does your event loop live?
4. How do you prevent race conditions in async ops?
5. What's your naming convention for functions?

FEATURE QUESTIONS:
1. For feature X, walk me through the code path
2. If I click this button, which functions run in what order?
3. What data is passed to which function?
4. Where is the return value stored?
5. What triggers the re-render?

EDGE CASE QUESTIONS:
1. What happens if user does X and Y simultaneously?
2. What if localStorage is full?
3. What if user's browser crashes mid-generation?
4. What if the same user opens 3 tabs?
5. What if user pastes 100,000 characters?

QUALITY QUESTIONS:
1. Which features are you most confident about?
2. Which features are you least confident about?
3. What would break if I add 10 more parts?
4. What would break if I add 50 sub-topics?
5. What's the largest file you tested?

HONESTY QUESTIONS:
1. What did you implement that wasn't asked?
2. What did you skip because it was too hard?
3. What did you fake with dummy data that should be real?
4. Are there any placeholder functions that don't actually work?
5. On a scale of 1-10, how production-ready is this?

STEP 2 — UNDERSTAND WHAT THE DOC DOES NOT MENTION
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The current 18-page doc is incomplete. During review, we discovered 
critical gaps that the doc does NOT cover. You must internalize these:

GAP 1 — DYNAMIC FEATURE DETECTION IS NOT A FLAT LIST
The doc shows Screen 3 as a simple toggle list of ~12 features 
(Current Affairs, PYQ, Formula Breakdown, etc.). This is WRONG.

Real content requires a 12-CATEGORY DYNAMIC BUNDLE SYSTEM with 
~130 sub-features:

1. 🗺️ Geography Bundle (15 sub-features)
   - Location maps, neighboring analysis, physical features, 
     climate zones, resources, connectivity, strategic importance, 
     historical rulers, cultural heritage, notable personalities 
     from place, current news, comparisons, route diagrams, etc.

2. 👥 Personalities Bundle (12 sub-features)
   - Bio cards, contemporaries, contributions timeline, works, 
     awards, influence network, places associated, quotes, 
     legacy, recent news, family/lineage trees, etc.

3. 🧮 Formula Operations Bundle (13 sub-features)
   - Not just showing the formula — but derivation steps, 
     formula facts (e.g., "if C↑ and I↓ by same amount, GDP 
     unchanged"), variable deep dive, substitution examples, 
     edge cases, unit analysis, formula family tree, sensitivity 
     analysis, common mistakes, real-world applications, 
     transformation, comparison tables, historical evolution

4. 📊 Chart/Data Operations Bundle (13 sub-features)
   - Auto-extract facts from data (e.g., "India GDP grew 8x in 
     20 years"), trend analysis, peak/valley detection, 
     percentage changes, correlation, anomaly detection, 
     what-if scenarios, chart type alternatives, data story, 
     reading skills, related data suggestions, Q&A, overlays

5. 🏛️ Administration/Governance Bundle (13 sub-features)
   - Org hierarchy, powers table, appointment flowchart, 
     term & tenure, salary, landmark decisions, constitutional 
     provisions, amendments timeline, international comparison, 
     committee reports, officeholders, institution network, 
     reform suggestions

6. 📖 Storytelling Bundle (10 sub-features)
   - Character stories (like Shyam bread), case studies, 
     analogies, historical events, day-in-the-life, contrasting 
     scenarios, hypotheticals, personal journeys, dialogue boxes, 
     village/city micro-scenarios

7. 📅 Historical Bundle (13 sub-features)
   - Master timeline, cause & effect chains, contemporary world 
     events, empire extent maps, dynasty trees, battle maps, 
     rise & fall analysis, architecture galleries, cultural 
     achievements, administrative structure, economy of era, 
     legacy box, sources of info

8. ⚖️ Legal/Constitutional Bundle (10 sub-features)
   - Article deep dive, landmark judgments, amendment history, 
     comparative constitution, enforcement mechanism, penalties, 
     exceptions, related laws network, controversies, committee 
     reports

9. 📰 Current Affairs Layers Bundle (12 sub-features)
   - 7-day recent, 30-day recent, 1-year recent, 5-year 
     landmarks, related places news, related personalities news, 
     international perspective, committee reports, government 
     schemes, upcoming events, controversies, data updates

10. 🔬 Scientific/Experiment Bundle (10 sub-features)
    - Experiment setup diagrams, procedure steps, expected 
      results, observations table, precautions, applications, 
      discoverer timeline, related experiments, extensions, 
      common errors

11. 🩺 Medical/Biological Bundle (10 sub-features)
    - Anatomy diagrams, physiological process flow, disease 
      progression, symptom checker, treatment comparison, drug 
      interactions, diagnostic flowcharts, case studies, 
      prevention, latest research

12. 💻 Computer Science Bundle (15 sub-features)
    - Code blocks, algorithm pseudocode, complexity analysis, 
      data structure visualizations, architecture diagrams, 
      UML, database schema, network topology, state machines, 
      design patterns, debugging walkthroughs, optimizations, 
      real-world systems, interview questions, common bugs

For each detected content type, the app must auto-suggest features 
from these bundles. Undetected bundles must show as [Force Enable] 
options — user can manually turn on any category.

GAP 2 — CONTENT DETECTION ENGINE IS UNDEFINED
The doc says "AI analyzes and detects features" but never explains 
HOW. The engine must detect:
- Location mentions → trigger geography bundle
- Person names → trigger personalities bundle
- Numerical data → trigger chart/data bundle
- Formulas → trigger formula operations bundle
- Time periods → trigger historical bundle
- Institutions → trigger administration bundle
- Abstract concepts → trigger storytelling bundle
- Legal references → trigger legal bundle
- Scientific terms → trigger science bundle
- Medical terms → trigger medical bundle
- Code/technical content → trigger CS bundle
- Process/creation → trigger process bundle

GAP 3 — PROMPT TRANSPARENCY IS MISSING FROM DOC
The doc mentions "prompt architecture" but never shows the actual 
prompts to users. Every AI operation must have a visible, 
copyable, editable prompt:
- Screen 3: Meta-prompt used to analyze source
- Screen 4: Meta-prompt used to split into parts
- Screen 5: Meta-prompt used to create each blueprint
- Screen 6: Prompt that will generate the notes
- Screen 7: Prompt for text generation per part

To: Project Lead / QA Team
Subject: CRITICAL QA ESCALATION — Major Flow Blockers & Routing Bugs in v2 Skeletons

Hi Lead,

While doing a deep-dive functional test on the StudyForge v2 Skeleton, I discovered several critical routing and UI bugs that completely block the user flow.

Before we proceed with grading the developers or merging any code, I need you to append these specific failure points to the master Verification Checklist and re-evaluate the builds.

CRITICAL: Please immediately check if the other developers (akshatpck and anilfamily) also have these same bugs in their submissions.

Here are the specific issues that need to be verified across all developer builds:

🚨 THE NEW VERIFICATION CHECKLIST (Append to Master List)
Please test the following interactions manually in the browser:

[ ] BUG 1: Screen 4 "Continue to Blueprint" is locked.

The Issue: Clicking the "Continue to Blueprint →" button at the bottom of Screen 4 throws a "Stage locked until prerequisites are satisfied" error.
The Cause: Developers used a strict navTo() function that checks against a maxScreen state, rather than using a direct navigate() override to advance the user to a newly unlocked screen. This completely breaks the app flow.
[ ] BUG 2: Screen 4 "Edit Settings" button is unresponsive/hidden.

The Issue: Clicking "✏️ Edit Settings" on any Part card in Screen 4 does not open a settings modal or configuration panel.
The Cause: It is currently just wired to a tiny toast notification (app.showNotification()) that vanishes in 3 seconds, making it look like a dead button. It needs a proper dummy UI modal to represent part configuration.
[ ] BUG 3: Cannot find the "Generated Blueprint Prompt".

The Issue: Users are unable to locate the actual AI generation prompt.
The Fix Required: The prompt does exist, but it is buried inside the Screen 6 Overlay. Because Screen 4's "Continue" button is broken (Bug 1), users cannot reach Screen 5 to click the cards that open Screen 6. The flow must be unblocked so users can find the "Syllabus Prompt Architecture" textarea.
[ ] BUG 4: Subsequent Progression Buttons are also broken.

The Issue: Due to the exact same routing logic flaw in Bug 1, if you manage to bypass Screen 4, the "Approve & Start Generation" button on Screen 5, and the "Finish & Go to Export" button on Screen 7 will also throw the "Stage locked" error.
[ ] BUG 5: HTML Escaping Crash in Custom Image Visualizer.

The Issue: On Screen 7, if a user highlights text, clicks "Visualize This", and types double quotes (") into the "Edit Prompt" box, it breaks the HTML rendering when the custom image block is injected into the notes.
[ ] BUG 6: "Add Part" button drops dynamic bundle data.

The Issue: Clicking "➕ Add Part" on Screen 4 successfully adds a new part. However, it fails to inherit or display the dynamic Feature Bundle icons (🗺️, 📊, etc.) on Screen 5, breaking the UI consistency.
Lead Action Items:

Re-run the validation test incorporating these 6 bugs.
Check the submissions from akshatpck and anilfamily immediately to see if their routing logic and "Edit Settings" buttons share these exact same failures.
Report back on which developer (if any) successfully bypassed the navTo lock logic for forward progression.
Let me know the results across all three builds so we can determine who needs to issue the next patch!I completely understand. I will not generate any code or overwrite the file in this response. Instead, I will break down exactly why you are experiencing these issues in the current skeleton build and identify a few other underlying bugs in the flow so we know exactly what needs to be fixed in the next iteration.

Here is the troubleshooting report based on the issues you found, plus a few others I caught while reviewing the logic:

1. "Continue to Blueprint is locked" & "I don't find the prerequisite"
The Cause: This is a routing bug in the skeleton's JavaScript.
The "Continue" button on Screen 4 uses onclick="app.navTo(5)".
However, the app.navTo() function contains a strict check: it only allows you to navigate to a screen if the screenNum is less than or equal to this.state.maxScreen. Because you are currently on Screen 4, the maxScreen is set to 4. When the button asks to go to 5, the app blocks it and fires the "Stage locked until prerequisites are satisfied" error.
The Solution (for later): Primary forward-moving "Continue" buttons at the bottom of screens need to bypass the strict navTo check and use app.navigate(nextScreen) directly to advance the maxScreen state.

2. "Edit setting is not clickable" (Stage 4)
The Cause: The button actually is clickable, but it doesn't open a popup. In the current skeleton, the ✏️ Edit Settings button is wired to app.showNotification('Configuring details...').
When you click it, a small dark toast notification pops up in the bottom-right corner of the screen for 3 seconds and fades away. Because it doesn't open a large modal, it feels like it's broken or unresponsive.
The Solution (for later): We need to either build a dummy "Settings Modal" overlay for Part configuration or make the toast notification much more obvious.

3. "I don't see the prompt generated for blueprint"
The Cause: The generated prompt is hidden inside the Blueprint Detail Overlay (Screen 6).
To see it, you have to successfully reach Screen 5 (which is currently blocked by the bug mentioned in #1). Once on Screen 5, you would click the "🔍 See full blueprint →" button on one of the cards. This opens a full-screen overlay, and inside that overlay under "Section 2: Synthesis Text Plan", there is a text box titled "Syllabus Prompt Architecture" containing the generated prompt.
Note: Because this is a dummy skeleton, the prompt is hardcoded to say "Write comprehensive notes on [Part Title]..." rather than actually generating from your Screen 1 inputs.

🔍 Further Troubleshooting (Other Bugs Identified in the Code)
While reviewing the code to figure out your issues, I found a few other bugs that will surface once the routing bug is fixed:

Bug A: The "Start Generation" button on Screen 5 will also fail.
Once you approve all blueprints and the "Approve & Start Generation" button unlocks, clicking it will trigger the exact same "Stage locked" error. It uses app.navTo(6) instead of app.navigate(6).
Bug B: The "Finish & Go to Export" button on Screen 7 will fail.
Same issue as above. It uses app.navTo(7) which will block you from reaching the final export screen.
Bug C: Image visualizer text escaping.
If you use double quotes (") inside the "Edit Prompt" box when generating a custom image on Screen 7, it will break the HTML rendering when it injects the custom image box into the DOM because the string isn't being sanitized.
Bug D: "Add Part" Dummy Data Sync.
If you click "➕ Add Part" on Screen 4, it correctly adds "Part 7" to the list. However, because it's a generic addition, it doesn't push the dynamic feature bundle icons (like the Geography or Data icons) to that new card on Screen 5, leaving its blueprint card looking slightly empty compared to the others.
Next Steps:
Let me know when you are ready! I can fix the navTo vs navigate routing logic so you can freely move through Screens 4, 5, 6, 7, and 8 without getting locked out, and I can wire up a visual modal for the "Edit Settings" button.
VERBAL SPOT-CHECK QUESTION ANSWERS
